Motivation: 现有参考式指标（CIDEr、SPICE）和LLM-as-scorer在验证密集事实性声明方面存在困难，而QA替代方案通常探针密度低、领域覆盖面窄，或缺乏问题与分割图像区域之间的显式对齐。需要一种更严谨、更具事实核查能力的详细描述评估方法。

Method: 将每张图像分解为覆盖前景和背景的粗粒度语义区域；对每个保留区域生成涵盖10个语义类别的多选题；基于37个L1域和219个L2子域的两层分类法组织；由语言评判器仅从描述中回答问题；引入Uncertain选项和Effective Accuracy指标进行判断；采用基于密度的指标惩罚冗长且无信息的描述；通过将无约束的标量评分转换为结构化MCQ阅读来降低成本。

Result: 在13个VLM上的实验显示了模型间巨大的Coverage差距、清晰的效能-效率权衡，以及稀疏或基于重叠的评估常常遗漏的失败模式。该协议成本效益高，减少了开放式评分偏差，在固定读者下产生相对稳定的模型排名。

Conclusion: CapProbe提供了一种区域对齐、高密度、全领域覆盖的密集QA评估协议，能够更可靠地区分模型回答质量，识别现有评估方法遗漏的失败模式，并支持成本效益高的模型排名。基准数据、标注和评估代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Evaluating detailed image captions from Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires going beyond surface-level semantic similarity. Reference-based metrics (e.g., CIDEr and SPICE) and LLM-as-scorer protocols struggle to verify dense factual claims, while existing QA-based alternatives generally offer lower probe density, narrower domain coverage, or no explicit alignment between individual questions and segmented image regions. We introduce CapProbe, a full-scene dense QA benchmark that turns detailed caption evaluation into region-aligned factual checking. Each image is decomposed into coarse semantic regions covering both foreground and background elements; for every retained region, we generate multiple-choice questions spanning 10 semantic categories, forming a dense checklist of probed visual facts. Guided by a two-tier taxonomy of 37 L1 domains and 219 L2 sub-domains, CapProbe comprises 346 images, 1,868 regions, and 25,650 questions, averaging 74 QA pairs per image. A language judge answers from the caption alone; an Uncertain option and Effective Accuracy provide a judge-dependent proxy for distinguishing unanswered probes from incorrectly resolved ones, while density-based metrics penalize verbose yet uninformative captions. The protocol is cost-effective: by converting unconstrained scalar scoring into structured MCQ reading, it reduces open-ended scoring bias while remaining judge-conditioned and yields relatively stable model rankings under a fixed reader. Experiments on 13 VLMs show large Coverage gaps across models, a clear competency-efficiency trade-off, and failure modes that sparse or overlap-based evaluation often misses. The benchmark data, annotations, and evaluation code will be released soon.

</details>


### [104] [Static in Frames, Dynamic in Events: Rethinking Features in Event Cameras as Motion Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11075)
*Hesam Araghi,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tomen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析事件相机角点检测中两个特征——结构张量特征值和时空密度值——发现它们本质上是运动线索，并证明将这些特征与局部几何信息结合可以增强事件光流估计的性能，在合成数据和真实数据集DSEC上均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机以异步方式捕捉强度变化，其数据天然编码场景动态和物体运动信息，与基于帧的静态快照有本质区别。现有工作尚未系统分析事件角点检测中特征的运动语义，也未充分利用这些特征来增强下游任务（如光流估计）的性能。

Method: 首先从理论上分析移动角点处结构张量特征值与运动方向之间的关系；随后在合成数据集上设计受控实验，验证将局部几何特征与特征值、密度值相结合的互补运动信息的有效性和鲁棒性（对纹理和散粒噪声）；最后将提出的特征整合进state-of-the-art的事件光流网络中，在真实DSEC基准上评估。

Result: 在DSEC真实基准上，加入特征后光流精度一致提升，在数据稀缺场景和低容量模型下提升最为显著，且对纹理和散粒噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 事件数据中看似用于角点检测的特征（结构张量特征值和时空密度值）实际上是运动线索，可作为一种有效的补充信号显著改善事件光流估计，尤其在小数据和低容量模型场景下收益最大。

Abstract: Event cameras capture intensity changes asynchronously with high temporal resolution, requiring novel preprocessing methods for downstream tasks. Unlike static intensity snapshots, event data inherently encode information about scene dynamics and object motion, meaning that features derived from events can exhibit behaviors with no direct analogue in frame-based vision. In this paper, we analyze two features used in event-based corner detection---the eigenvalues of the structure tensor and the spatiotemporal density values---and show that they are \emph{motion cues}. We hypothesize that these features, combined with local geometric information, can enhance motion estimation tasks. To validate this, we first theoretically analyze how the eigenvalues of the structure tensor at moving corner points relate to the direction of motion. We then design controlled experiments on a synthetic dataset, confirming that extending local geometric features with eigenvalues and density values provides complementary motion information and is robust to texture and shot noise. Finally, we integrate the proposed features into a state-of-the-art event-based optical flow network and evaluate on the real-world DSEC benchmark, where the added features consistently improve accuracy, with the largest gains in data-scarce scenarios and for lower-capacity models. The code for this paper can be found at: \href{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}.

</details>


### [105] [Foundation Model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling (FEEDS): A label-efficient training strategy for pan-cancer, multi-tracer PET/CT datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11076)
*Biratal Raj Wagle,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Grant Chau,Matthew E. Maeder,Muhammad Azeem Arshad,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEEDS是一种基于视觉基础模型嵌入的标签与计算高效学习策略，用于全身PET/CT病灶分割。它通过选择最具信息量和多样性的未标注样本进行专家标注，仅需有限的代表性训练集即可匹配全标注（100%）训练的性能，同时减少70%的标注负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT病灶自动分割需要大量高质量标注数据，但创建这些标注既耗时又需要专业知识。有限标注数据训练的模型在准确性和泛化性上不足，难以满足临床需求。现有无监督、半监督和主动学习方法均存在各自的局限性。

Method: FEEDS利用视觉基础模型嵌入来从大量未标注的临床数据中筛选最具信息量和多样性的病例用于专家标注。与无监督、半监督和主动学习方法不同，FEEDS采用一步式训练范式，仅需有限的代表性训练集即可完成训练，兼顾标签和计算效率。训练和验证使用AutoPET-III数据集，并在AutoPET-III、DeepPSMA及内部Dartmouth-Hitchcock医学中心数据集三个留置集上测试。

Result: FEEDS在体素、病灶和解剖区域三个层面均优于基于随机采样的标注方法、基于伪标签的半监督学习以及仅用有限标注数据训练的方法。它在三个测试集上均实现良好泛化，覆盖FDG和PSMA两种示踪剂及多种疾病，仅用30%的标注数据即可匹配全标注（100%）训练性能。

Conclusion: FEEDS有效解决了病灶自动分割框架中的标签稀缺问题，为大容量未注释临床数据构建代表性和多样性标注队列提供了实用方案，在降低标注成本的同时保持临床级分割性能。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT imaging can assist clinicians with cancer detection, staging, and treatment planning across radiotracers and cancer types. However, training lesion segmentation models that capture variations in lesion size, distribution, and appearance requires large annotated datasets, whose creation is both time- and expertise-intensive. As a result, models trained on limited labeled PET/CT data often lack the accuracy and generalizability needed for clinical use. We present FEEDS (Foundation model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling), a label- and compute-efficient learning strategy that uses vision foundation model embeddings to select the most informative and diverse unlabeled cases for expert annotation. Unlike unsupervised, semi-supervised, and active learning approaches, FEEDS is a one-step training paradigm requiring only a limited, representative training set, making it label- and compute-efficient. We train and validate FEEDS using the AutoPET-III dataset. We test its accuracy and generalizability on three held-out sets: AutoPET-III, DeepPSMA, and an internal Dartmouth-Hitchcock Medical Center dataset. We evaluate clinical utility at the voxel, lesion, and anatomic region level to assess performance in high-risk areas and treatment planning utility. FEEDS outperforms random-sampling-based labeling, pseudolabel-based semi-supervised learning, and training with limited labeled data alone. It generalizes across all three test sets, FDG and PSMA tracers, and multiple diseases, matching fully-labeled (100\%) training performance with 70\% less annotation burden. FEEDS addresses the challenge of label scarcity in an automatic lesion segmentation framework by providing a practical approach for constructing representative and diverse annotation queues from large, unannotated clinical repositories.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Gaussian Structure: Intervention-Guided Density Control for Feed-Forward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11077)
*Hang Li,Jiahe Li,Meiying Gu,Jin Zheng,Lina Yu,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LGS框架通过学习高斯剪枝与增密策略（Densify Policy）和跨时间点查询机制，在推理时直接调整高斯结构并增强属性预测，在Waymo和PandaSet数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的前馈高斯重建方法保留了观测点与高斯原语间的初始对应关系，将初始化集合视为最终表示，无法像优化式3DGS那样通过训练累积梯度来决定表示如何增密；同时共享稀疏骨干网络仅隐式融合不同时间戳的观测，缺乏对单个原语的跨时间证据的显式聚合。

Method: 1) 提出Gaussian Densify Policy：通过剪枝或添加干预引发的局部梯度响应变化，判断结构调整是否利于重建，学习包含Prune和Addition分数的Densify Map，在推理时直接调整高斯结构。2) 提出紧凑的Cross-Time Point Query：从其他时间戳的高斯原语中显式检索并聚合邻近特征，用于可靠的属性预测。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上的大量实验表明，LGS在重建质量上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式的结构学习（剪枝与增密策略）和跨时间特征聚合，前馈高斯重建可以克服初始化表示不可变的局限，实现更优的场景重建效果。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [107] [Every Packet Counts: Dispersing Information for Loss-Resilient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11096)
*Yuhang Wei,Chuqin Zhou,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的抗丢包图像压缩方案，通过通道间能量重分布（ICR）和交错通道分组（ICG）解决信息分布不均问题，并采用两层双分支自回归结构缩短解码依赖链，在20%丢包率下PSNR比现有方法高1.84dB，且对突发性丢包具有良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型图像压缩方法虽然在率失真性能上表现出色，但在卫星通信和应急通信等场景中极易受到数据包丢失的影响。这种脆弱性源于两个层面：打包阶段信息分布不均匀导致关键信息集中在少数通道，以及熵编码阶段存在顺序解码依赖导致误差级联传播。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR)机制在打包前重新分配通道能量，防止关键信息集中于少数通道；(2) Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG)策略采用跨步方式划分潜在通道，将信息分散到各数据包中并控制在受限大小内；(3) 两层双分支自回归结构缩短依赖链，限制丢包导致的级联错误传播。

Result: 实验表明该方法在重建质量和稳定性上均优于现有方法。在20%丢包率下，平均PSNR比LossResilientLIC提升1.84dB，PSNR方差降低一个数量级。仅在均匀随机丢包下训练，就能泛化到Gilbert-Elliott信道建模的突发性丢包场景，且优于专门针对该场景训练的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过信息重分布、分组分散和依赖链缩短三个层面的协同设计，有效解决了学习型图像压缩在丢包环境下的脆弱性问题，同时展现出从均匀丢包到突发丢包的良好泛化能力，适用于卫星和应急通信等实际场景。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved impressive rate-distortion performance. However, existing methods remain highly vulnerable to packet loss, a common challenge in satellite and emergency communications. This vulnerability stems from non-uniform information distribution at the packetization stage and sequential decoding dependencies at the entropy coding stage. We propose an end-to-end loss-resilient image compression scheme that addresses both. Before packetization, we introduce an Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR) mechanism to redistribute channel energy, preventing critical information concentrating in a small subset of channels. Then, an Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG) strategy partitions latent channels in a strided manner to disperse information across packets, with each packet kept within constrained sizes. To limit cascading errors from lost packets, we adopt a two-layer dual-branch autoregressive structure to shorten the dependency chain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in both reconstruction quality and stability. At 20% packet loss, it achieves an average PSNR gain of 1.84 dB over LossResilientLIC while reducing PSNR variance by an order of magnitude. Notably, trained under uniform random loss only, our model generalizes to bursty loss modeled by the Gilbert-Elliott channel, outperforming methods explicitly trained for such conditions.

</details>


### [108] [AlbumentationsX: One Augmentation Pipeline for Images and Related Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11123)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumentationsX是一个数据增强库，通过将所有变换、概率、标注设置和随机种子统一封装在Compose对象中，确保图像及其所有标注（掩码、边界框、关键点、立体视图、视频帧、体积等）在每次调用时共享同一组随机值，从根本上避免因随机变化不一致而导致的训练样本数据错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据增强库中，图像和其对应标注可能分别接收不同的随机变换（如裁剪坐标不一致），导致掩码、边界框、关键点等标注与图像错位，从而损坏训练样本。这种随机值分开选择的代码路径会静默地导致数据不对齐。

Method: AlbumentationsX将所有变换列表、概率、标注设置和随机种子集中于一个Compose对象中。每次调用时随机值仅选择一次，并统一应用于训练样本的所有受支持部分。库将每个对象的掩码、边界框和标签保持在一起，并支持项目自定义变换，同时能够保存pipeline定义、可视化单次调用的处理过程并复现该次调用。

Result: 该库的应用示例如下：Compose放置在文件解码为数组之后、PyTorch将样本组成批之前。AlbumentationsX执行声明的变换，同时保持图像与所有标注的对齐一致性。

Conclusion: AlbumentationsX通过在统一的Compose对象中管理随机性，解决了数据增强中图像与标注不同步的核心问题。但翻转、裁剪、颜色变化等操作是否保留正确标签的最终决策权仍交由实践者根据具体任务判断。

Abstract: Augmentation can corrupt a training example when an image and its annotations receive different random changes. A crop must use the same coordinates for the image, mask, boxes, keypoints, stereo views, video frames, or volume. Code paths that choose these values separately can silently misalign the data.
  AlbumentationsX keeps the transform list, probabilities, annotation settings, and random seed in one Compose object. Each call chooses random values once and applies them to every supported part of the training example. The library keeps each object's mask, box, and label together and lets projects add their own transforms. It can also save the pipeline definition, show what happened in one call, and run that call again.
  The examples place Compose after files have been decoded into arrays and before PyTorch groups examples into a batch. AlbumentationsX executes the declared transforms. Practitioners still decide whether a flip, crop, color change, or other operation preserves the correct label for their task.

</details>


### [109] [Is There Really a Camouflaged Object? Towards Realistic Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11135)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si,Wende Tan,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPC16K大规模基准与OPCNet模型，解决开放世界下伪装目标检测（COD）的假阳性问题。OPC16K包含16,245张图像，覆盖伪装目标、纯背景和非伪装目标三类场景；OPCNet将COD从纯分割任务重构为"目标定位+伪装存在推理"的联合问题，通过分层存在推理、相似性感知伪装关系建模和存在感知特征细化，在保持分割精度的同时显著降低负样本上的假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法大多基于封闭世界假设，假定每张输入图像都包含伪装目标，忽略了纯背景或非伪装目标的现实场景，导致模型在实际开放环境中产生大量假阳性。这一局限亟需解决。

Method: （1）构建OPC16K基准：包含16,245张图像、14个来源，分为伪装目标/纯背景/非伪装目标三类图像，支持分割质量与负样本拒绝能力的综合评估。（2）提出OPCNet：将COD重定义为目标定位与伪装存在推理的联合问题，包含三个核心组件——分层存在推理区分CO/BG/NOCOD三类场景、相似性感知伪装关系建模捕获前景-背景伪装线索、存在感知特征细化用存在预测调控分割特征。

Result: 在OPC16K上的大量实验表明，OPCNet在该现实性COD评估协议下取得优越性能，在负样本上显著减少假阳性，同时保持准确的伪装目标分割能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过提出包含负样本的基准和新颖的感知存在性的网络架构，突破了传统COD的封闭世界假设，为实现现实开放环境下可靠的伪装目标检测提供了有效的基准和方法。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that are visually concealed in their surroundings and has attracted increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing COD methods are developed under a closed-world assumption, where each input image is assumed to contain a camouflaged object. This assumption ignores realistic scenarios with pure backgrounds or non-camouflaged objects, causing existing models to produce severe false positives when deployed in open-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose OPC16K, a large-scale benchmark for realistic COD. OPC16K contains 16,245 images from 14 sources and is carefully organized into camouflaged-object images, pure background images, and non-camouflaged-object images, enabling comprehensive evaluation of both segmentation quality and negative-sample rejection. Based on this benchmark, we further propose OPCNet, a presence-aware camouflage network that reformulates COD from a pure segmentation task into a joint problem of object localization and camouflage existence reasoning. Specifically, OPCNet introduces hierarchical existence reasoning to distinguish CO, BG, and NOCOD scenarios, similarity-aware camouflage relation modeling to capture foreground-background camouflage cues, and existence-aware feature refinement to regulate segmentation features with existence predictions. Extensive experiments on OPC16K demonstrate that OPCNet achieves superior performance under the proposed realistic COD evaluation protocol, significantly reducing false positives on negative samples while maintaining accurate camouflaged-object segmentation. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/OPCOD.

</details>


### [110] [SAR2Agri: Learning SAR Intensity Representations for Agricultural Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11142)
*Moti Rattan Gupta,Anupam Sobti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个仅使用SAR强度影像进行农业应用的自监督学习流水线，通过掩码和课程学习改进时间基预训练任务，在SICKLE基准上达到84.9% IoU，超越光学和现有SAR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业监测面临复杂的时间、物候和气候动态挑战，对粮食安全至关重要。尽管SAR卫星具备全天候成像能力，但现有SAR基础模型主要关注目标检测、洪水测绘和土地覆盖分类等应用，缺乏针对农业下游任务的专用预训练方法。现有多模态基础模型依赖光学影像进行grounding，而近期工作中基于物候的时间预训练任务仅使用光学影像，尚未探索仅使用SAR强度影像的预训练流水线。

Method: 提出首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习流水线，通过掩码（masking）和课程学习（curriculum learning）改进物候启发的时间预训练任务，以增强预训练流水线从SAR数据中捕获物候特征的能力。

Result: 在SICKLE基准上，最终模型在作物类型映射任务上达到84.9% IoU，超越光学基线15.3个百分点，超越现有SAR基线2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于SAR强度编码器的预训练流水线对农业监测任务的有效性，填补了SAR专用预训练模型在农业应用领域的空白。

Abstract: Agricultural monitoring faces unique challenges, arising from the landscape's complex temporal, phenological, and climate dynamics, yet monitoring them is critical for ensuring food security. Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites offer all-weather day-night imaging capability supporting key monitoring tasks including crop type mapping, yield prediction and phenological event detection. Existing multimodal remote sensing foundation models including TerraMind and CopernicusFM learn SAR representations by grounding them in optical imagery using joint encoding and contrastive learning techniques, while SAR-specific foundation models such as SAR-JEPA, SARMAE, and SAR-W-MixMAE primarily focus on target detection, flood mapping, and land cover classification applications. Recent work has introduced phenology inspired temporal pretext tasks with optical imagery which has shown strong performance on agricultural downstream tasks. In this work, we propose the first self-supervised learning pipeline focused on using only SAR intensity imagery for agricultural applications. We improve the temporal pretext tasks through masking and curriculum learning to enhance the pretraining pipeline's ability to capture phenological features from SAR. On the SICKLE benchmark, our final model achieves 84.9% IoU on crop type mapping, outperforming optical baselines (by 15.3 pt) and existing SAR baselines (by 2.2 pt), demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed pipeline for pretraining SAR intensity encoders for agricultural monitoring.

</details>


### [111] [PRMU: A Corpus-Free Benchmark for Person-Centric Knowledge Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11149)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Ziyuan Chen,Wenda Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRMU基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在无原始语料（corpus-free）条件下的真实人物中心知识删除场景。同时提出SGPE（Similarity-Gated Projection Editing）轻量级无语料删除基线方法，实验表明现有方法在遗忘与局部性保持之间存在不利权衡且易受多模态知识重激活攻击，而SGPE在目标遗忘、局部性保持和通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs的机器遗忘方法通常假设能访问原始的遗忘语料和保留语料，但在现实删除场景中这些语料往往不可获得。需要一种更符合实际场景的、无原始语料约束的多模态遗忘评估方法。

Method: 1) PRMU基准：聚焦自然习得的人物相关知识，通过多样化的文本和视觉探针（含对抗性评估和细粒度局部性分析）评估模型能否删除目标知识同时保留相关知识；2) SGPE方法：结合知识位移、受保护参数空间编辑和局部性感知多模态控制的相似性门控投影编辑轻量级无语料遗忘基线方法。

Result: 在对代表性MLLMs的大量实验中发现：现有遗忘方法常常遭受不利的遗忘-局部性权衡，在激进遗忘设置下局部性显著退化，并且对多模态知识重激活攻击脆弱。SGPE在目标遗忘、局部性保持和通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的权衡。

Conclusion: PRMU可作为现实且可扩展的多模态机器遗忘研究的基准平台，推动未来在该方向上的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/PRMU.

</details>


### [112] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding,Meilu Song,Yun Chen,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CausalSplat框架，将视觉-语言模型与3D场景图结合，用于推理3D高斯分割任务。并构建Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet两个基准，评估常识、空间、可供性与反事实推理能力，实验证明该方法在推理基准和标准3D分割任务上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅的开放词汇场景理解方法仅限于显式查询，无法处理实际具身交互所需的隐式意图、复杂空间约束和常识推理。

Method: CausalSplat框架通过将视觉-语言模型与3D场景图集成，将显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理进行解耦。

Result: 在推理基准上达到最先进性能，并在标准指代分割和开放词汇3D分割任务上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 解耦显式感知与隐式推理的架构设计能有效解决推理3D高斯分割问题，为具身智能与3D场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page: https://jiayuding031020.github.io/CausalSplat

</details>


### [113] [MultiModal Code-Switching: Interleaving Visual Objects into Language for Explicit Object-Level Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11167)
*Changhao Xiang,Shangyu Xing,Zhen Wu,Jianbing Zhang,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS)预训练范式，通过用视觉对象替换文本实体来提供显式的对象级监督，解决多模态大语言模型图像级对齐中的指称歧义问题，仅用50K样本即可匹敌或超越基于600K图像-文本对训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要依赖图像-文本对进行模态对齐预训练，将全局图像表示映射到长文本描述，但这种图像级对齐存在指称歧义问题——模型难以从全局表示中推断多个视觉对象与文本实体之间的对应关系，导致数据效率低下和语义接地能力欠佳。

Method: 受语言中代码转换（code-switching）现象启发，MMCS将文本中的实体替换为其对应的视觉对象，交错视觉与语言信息，从而强制执行局部视觉-语言接地。同时开发了可扩展的数据合成流水线，生成了包含773K样本、具有精确对象-实体对应关系的预训练数据集。

Result: 实验表明MMCS具有极高的数据效率：仅用50K样本即可匹配或超越600K图像-文本对的训练效果；并且在不同模型规模下一致提升了视觉接地和感知能力。

Conclusion: MMCS通过提供显式的对象级监督，有效解决了图像级对齐的指称歧义问题，显著提升了MLLMs的数据效率与视觉接地能力，为多模态预训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on image-text pairs for modality alignment pretraining, mapping global image representations to long textual descriptions. However, this image-level alignment suffers from referential ambiguity: models struggle to infer the correspondences between multiple visual objects and textual entities from the global representation, leading to data inefficiency and suboptimal semantic grounding. To address this, we propose MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS), a novel pretraining paradigm that provides explicit object-level supervision. Inspired by the linguistic phenomenon of code-switching, MMCS interleaves vision and language by replacing textual entities with their corresponding visual objects, enforcing local vision-language grounding. We further develop a scalable data synthesis pipeline to generate a pretraining dataset of 773K samples with accurate object-entity correspondences. Experiments show that MMCS is highly data-efficient: with only 50K samples, it matches or surpasses models trained on 600K image-text pairs. Furthermore, MMCS consistently improves visual grounding and perception capabilities across varying model scales.

</details>


### [114] [Test-Time Self-Evolving GUI Visual Grounding via Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11191)
*Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个测试时自进化框架（Test-Time Self-Evolving），用于GUI视觉定位，使模型在部署后无需人工标注即可自主改进。框架通过探索、评估、反思、内化的闭环，结合MLLM-based Reflector、Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation和Contrastive Calibration，在六个基准上平均提升7.4%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉定位模型在部署后参数冻结，难以适应未见过的界面。虽然已有方法尝试通过测试时强化学习进行适应，但这些方法无法对失败的探索进行反思，限制了模型的自我改进能力。

Method: 提出测试时自进化框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) 探索阶段，代理预测未见界面的定位坐标；(2) 引入基于MLLM的Reflector评估生成结果并提供推理反思；(3) 提出Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation，通过条件自教师将高层推理转化为密集的token级监督信号；(4) 设计Contrastive Calibration方法，防止错误的自动回归前缀在失败探索中破坏监督信号。

Result: 在六个基准上进行广泛实验，平均准确率较基础模型提升7.4%。这是首个成功将on-policy自蒸馏应用于GUI视觉定位测试时自适应的工作。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了GUI智能体部署后自适应能力的空白，完成了GUI智能体的自进化能力闭环。代码将开源发布。

Abstract: GUI Visual Grounding is a fundamental capability for GUI agents. Existing models typically freeze their parameters after deployment, limiting their ability to adapt to unseen interfaces. Although recent methods attempt to adapt models via test-time reinforcement learning, they cannot reflect upon failed exploration. To overcome this, we propose a Test-Time Self-Evolving framework that enables models to improve after deployment without human-annotated ground truth. It constructs a closed-loop of Exploration, Evaluation, Reflection, and Internalization. Specifically, the agent first explores unseen interfaces by predicting grounding coordinates for given instructions. To evaluate these explorations, we introduce an MLLM-based Reflector to assess the generated results and provide the corresponding reasoning reflections. To internalize reflection knowledge into the model weights, we propose Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, which translates high-level reasoning into dense token-level supervision via a conditioned self-teacher. Furthermore, we design a Contrastive Calibration method to prevent incorrect auto-regressive prefixes from corrupting the supervisory signals during failed explorations. Extensive experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate our framework's effectiveness, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 7.4% over the base model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to successfully exploit on-policy self-distillation for test-time adaptation in GUI visual grounding. By filling the gap in post-deployment adaptation, our framework completes the self-evolving capability of GUI agents. The code will be released.

</details>


### [115] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu,Zheng Lu,Yuhan Bian,Xinchen Zhang,Xingming Shui,Yuesheng Huang,Xuhuan Li,Zihao Liu,Yifan Yang,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将元检测（meta-detection）引入AI生成视频检测领域，提出VidForensics-M1框架，通过证据导向的奖励重分配机制（Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution）在强化学习中联合优化预测标签和可验证的时间证据（temporal grounding），并设计了自动数据构建流程生成配对真假视频，实现鲁棒且可泛化的AI生成视频检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的AI生成视频检测器主要依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度的监督信号限制了模型对未见场景和新涌现视频生成器的泛化能力。文本理性（textual rationales）作为证据信号依赖外部模型生成，易受幻觉和语义偏差影响。因此需要引入更客观、可验证的证据形式（时间定位）以及更有效的证据整合机制。

Method: 1) 首次将元检测范式引入AI生成视频检测，在强化学习中联合优化预测标签与支持性证据。2) 提出自动数据构建管线，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段，生成配对的真假视频。3) 提出Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution机制，根据证据质量在标签正确的响应中重新分配奖励，实现证据感知的信用分配，保留可靠的标签监督同时鼓励模型获取细粒度且可验证的伪造定位能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，VidForensics-M1有效利用可验证的时间证据，在AI生成视频检测上取得了鲁棒且可泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 相比依赖外部模型生成文本理性（易受幻觉影响）或仅使用粗粒度标签监督的现有方法，引入客观可验证的时间定位证据并结合证据导向的奖励重分配，能够显著提升AI生成视频检测器的泛化能力和伪造定位能力，为AI视频取证提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction. Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [116] [Capturing Uncertainty in Human Motion for Representation Learning in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11203)
*Yizhou Xu,Lars Bretzner,Tiesheng Wang,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于未来运动预测的自监督表示学习框架，用于足球场景中的3D骨架人体运动理解，通过条件模块对离散化未来运动的概率分布进行建模，显著提升了运动预测精度并展现出良好的跨任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动本质上具有不确定性，仅预测单一未来轨迹不足以捕捉运动的底层动力学。为了学习有效的运动表示，需要对多个可能的未来进行建模，从而更好地理解3D骨架运动。

Method: 采用自监督表示学习框架，以未来运动预测为学习目标；引入一个条件模块，在3D欧几里得空间中对离散化未来运动的概率分布进行建模，利用未来轨迹作为显式监督信号来学习运动的多模态特性。

Result: 在大型足球运动员跟踪数据上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了运动预测精度；学习到的表示能够有效迁移到多个足球下游应用任务中，展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过对未来运动多模态分布的建模，自监督预测任务能够学习到鲁棒且可迁移的3D骨架运动表示，在足球场景中验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [117] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao,Jingkai Zhou,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了对抗性Fréchet距离（AdvFD），通过引入可学习的对抗表示与真实特征白化，解决Fréchet距离后训练中因静态预训练特征空间导致的Fréchet hacking问题，在一步生成器后训练上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet距离作为分布级目标用于生成器后训练时，直接优化会导致Fréchet hacking——目标指标持续提升但视觉质量和在其他特征空间中的Fréchet对齐停滞或恶化。根本原因是现有Fréchet损失使用的静态预训练特征空间只能提供不完整且固定的真实/生成分布差异视图。

Method: AdvFD在原有静态Fréchet目标基础上，引入一个可学习的对抗表示来补充静态表示目标。该可学习表示通过对抗方式最大化真实样本与生成样本之间的Fréchet差异，而生成器则在该自适应特征空间中最小化同一差异。为防止对抗表示通过特征放大平凡地增大目标，引入真实特征白化（real-feature whitening），归一化其尺度和协方差几何，稳定min-max优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD在JiT和pMF两种骨干网络上、不同模型规模下，均能一致地改进一步生成器后训练的Fréchet对齐和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过引入对抗学习的自适应特征表示并配合真实特征白化，可以有效缓解Fréchet hacking问题，验证了自适应特征空间相比静态特征空间在分布级生成器后训练中的优势。

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Algorithmic statistics of retinal images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09989)
*Loan Huynh,Ronald Zambrano,Layton Aho,Fabio Lavinsky,Gadi Wollstein,Joel S. Schuman,Andrew R. Cohen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于归一化压缩距离（NCD）和各项异性结构增强滤波器的度量学习方法，用于定量和可视化3D视网膜OCT图像中的疾病进展差异，预测视力场变化误差约为0.5 dB，优于非度量深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜OCT影像具有大、复杂、三维且难以有效可视化的特点。现有监督学习方法（如神经网络）是非度量的，可能引入与潜在无意义的生理差异相关的系统性失真，无法准确度量疾病进展。

Method: 采用归一化压缩距离（NCD）结合各向异性结构增强滤波器，对比3D视网膜图像对之间的结构差异；提出归一化压缩向量（NCV）作为特征集来度量图像集合间的视觉差异。

Result: 在验证NCD测量的结构差异与医生测量的视野功能变化之间的一致性时，预测误差约为0.5 dB，比非度量深度学习方法更准确。该方法成功应用于中度非进展性青光眼患者和非人类灵长类动物眼压操控模型中。模拟试验表明非度量嵌入特征（如神经网络产生的）会引入类别相关的统计失真。

Conclusion: 度量学习方法能够更准确、更可靠地量化和可视化3D视网膜OCT图像中的疾病进展模式，避免非度量方法引入的系统性统计失真。

Abstract: There has been a tremendous amount of image processing and machine learning research to measure and classify disease progression from live optical coherence tomography (OCT) imaging of the retina. The images considered here are large, complex, three-dimensional (3-D) and difficult to visualize effectively. Many current supervised machine learning approaches, \emph{e.g.} neural networks, are non-metric meaning that any features or measurements generated can introduce systematic distortion that may be correlated with underlying non-meaningful physiological differences. Here we present a metric learning approach using the normalized compression distance (NCD) combined with anisotropic structure-enhancing filters to quantify and visualize the principal differences among a collection of 3-D retinal images. We validate the NCD-measured structural differences between pairs of images against the physician-measured change in visual field function, achieving a prediction error of $\sim$ 0.5 dB, more accurate than non-metric deep learning approaches. The normalized compression vectors (NCV) are proposed as a feature set measuring visual differences among a collection of 3-D microscopy images. The utility of the NCV for visualizing and measuring patterns of change is demonstrated for a human with moderate non-progressing glaucoma and for a non-human primate model using intraocular pressure setting manipulation. We conclude with a brief simulation of non-metric embedding features, \emph{e.g.} from neural networks, introducing class-correlated statistical distortion.

</details>


### [119] [Longitudinal 3D Foundation Modeling for Neoadjuvant Breast Cancer Response Prediction from Serial DCE-MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09991)
*Fidel Omar Tito Cruz,Neda Ghafouri,Zengyan Wang,Pegah Khosravi,Yu Tian,Chen Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个纵向时间框架，结合冻结的3D基础编码器（Pillar-0）与时间动态网络（TDN），利用从治疗前到手术前的四个临床时间点的动态对比增强MRI序列来预测乳腺癌新辅助化疗后的病理完全缓解（pCR）。在982名患者的I-SPY2和ACRIN-6698联合队列上，模型在融合纵向3D影像与临床数据时实现AUROC 73.6%、平衡准确率69.1%，表明纵向影像能提供互补的预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pCR是新辅助化疗中的重要疗效终点，而现有的影像学预测方法大多基于单个静态时间点，无法捕捉治疗过程中肿瘤的动态变化，因此需要一种能够利用多时间点影像信息来改善pCR预测的纵向框架。

Method: 提出纵向框架，由冻结的3D基础编码器Pillar-0提取每次DCE-MRI的体积嵌入，再通过时间动态网络（TDN）融合跨四个临床时间点的时间感知体积嵌入，同时结合临床与治疗数据共同预测pCR。

Result: 在982名患者的I-SPY2和ACRIN-6698合并队列中，融合纵向3D影像与临床数据后取得测试AUROC 73.6%，平衡准确率69.1%。临床变量单独提供最强预测信号，但纵向3D影像在与临床数据融合时提供互补信息并改善pCR预测。

Conclusion: 纵向3D影像与临床数据的融合能够有效提升乳腺癌新辅助化疗后pCR的预测性能，表明跨时间点动态影像信息对治疗反应评估具有补充价值。

Abstract: Pathologic complete response (pCR) is an important endpoint in neoadjuvant chemotherapy (NAC) for breast cancer, and predicting pCR from imaging during treatment could support treatment response assessment. Many existing imaging-based approaches rely on a single static timepoint, which fails to capture changes that occur during treatment. In this work, we present a longitudinal framework that combines a frozen 3D foundation encoder (Pillar-0) with our Temporal Dynamics Network (TDN) to predict treatment response from serial Dynamic Contrast-Enhanced (DCE) MRI acquired across four clinical timepoints from pre-treatment to pre-surgery. The TDN combines time-aware volumetric embeddings with clinical and treatment data to predict pCR. Evaluated on 982 patients from the combined I-SPY2 and ACRIN-6698 cohort, the proposed model achieves strong performance across all reported metrics when longitudinal 3D imaging is fused with clinical data (test AUROC: 73.6%, balanced accuracy: 69.1%). While clinical variables provide the strongest individual predictive signal, longitudinal 3D imaging contributes complementary information when fused with clinical data, improving pCR prediction. Our source code is available at: https://github.com/omarftt/longitudinal_temporal_pillar.

</details>


### [120] [Knowledge-Guided 3D CT Generation: A Conditioning-Centric Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09992)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Eugenio Lomurno,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以条件信息为中心的分类体系（K x I x A），从外部知识类型、知识整合范式和生成架构三个正交维度对3D CT图像的条件生成文献进行系统性梳理，识别主导趋势与未充分探索的设计空间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT条件生成的现有研究发展迅速但方法多样，缺乏统一的比较框架使得系统对比困难，关键设计选择被掩盖，需要一个统一视角来组织和理解该领域的文献。

Method: 提出条件中心分类法，沿三个正交维度对文献进行分类：外部知识类型（K）、知识整合范式（I）和生成架构（A），构建显式的设计空间（K x I x A），并基于此框架系统化整理现有方法。

Result: 通过该框架系统化整理了现有的3D CT条件生成方法，识别出主导趋势和反复出现的设计模式，并指出了设计空间中尚未充分探索的区域以及有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 条件中心分类法（K x I x A）为3D CT条件生成提供了一个统一的结构化视角，有助于系统性比较现有工作、发现研究空白，并为未来在数据增强、隐私保护和临床场景模拟等方面的条件生成研究指明方向。

Abstract: Controllable generation guided by external knowledge is a key requirement in modern generative deep learning applications, enabling the synthesis of samples with explicit constraints on semantic content, structural properties, and variability. In 3D Computed Tomography (CT), such control is essential for clinical applications, including data augmentation, privacy-preserving data sharing, and the simulation of specific anatomical or pathological scenarios. While research on conditional 3D CT generation has expanded rapidly, the diversity of existing approaches makes systematic comparison difficult and obscures fundamental design choices. In this survey, we propose a conditioning-centric taxonomy that organizes the literature along three orthogonal dimensions: the type of external knowledge (K), the knowledge integration paradigm (I), and the generative architecture (A). This factorization defines an explicit design space (K x I x A) that provides a unified perspective on prior work. Using this framework, we systematize existing methods, identify dominant trends and recurring design patterns, and highlight underexplored regions of the design space that point toward promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [121] [APCReg: Anatomical-Prior-Guided Coarse-to-Fine CBCT--IOS Registration via Multi-View Projection and Reliability-Controlled Residual Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09993)
*Xincan Zheng,Yaqi Wang,Zhi Li,Jiahao Bao,Lan Feng,Yiru Xia,Shuai Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出APCReg，一个基于解剖先验引导的粗到细框架，用于CBCT与口内扫描（IOS）之间的全局配准与可靠性控制的残差校正，在60对测试下颌上实现了亚毫米级平均Chamfer距离（0.87mm）和2.92mm的Hausdorff距离，在六个评估指标中均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT与IOS之间的配准对个性化手术规划至关重要，但不同成像模态、有限的重叠区域和较大的姿态偏移导致自动配准不可靠，临床实践仍依赖传统几何流程和人工调整，因此需要更鲁棒的自动化配准方法。

Method: APCReg框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 多视角解剖粗配准（MACR）——通过颊侧、近中、咬合三个有序正交投影对齐来分解六自由度搜索，再进行三维细调；(2) 重叠感知残差配准（OARR）——结合共享KPConv特征、折叠弓长线索、重叠门控交叉注意力以及Sinkhorn匹配；(3) 牙弓结构假设选择——在可靠的保留对应点上评估多样位姿，并通过无真值粗保留守卫条件，在几何可靠时条件性保留粗配准结果。

Result: 在60对保留的下颌数据上，APCReg取得0.87mm的平均Chamfer距离和2.92mm的Hausdorff距离，在开源的基准方法比较中，六个报告指标均排名第一。

Conclusion: APCReg通过解剖先验引导的粗到细策略和可靠性控制机制，有效解决了CBCT与IOS跨模态配准中模态差异大、重叠有限和姿态偏移大的挑战，实现了亚毫米级的配准精度，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Registration between cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) and intraoral scans (IOS) is essential for patient-specific surgical planning. However, disparate imaging modalities, limited overlap, and large pose offsets make automated registration unreliable. Consequently, clinical registration remains dependent on conventional geometry pipelines and manual clinician adjustment. To address these challenges, we propose APCReg, an anatomical-prior-guided coarse-to-fine framework for global registration and reliability-controlled residual correction. Specifically, multi-view anatomical coarse registration (MACR) performs ordered orthogonal projection alignment (buccal, proximal, and occlusal) to decompose the six-degree-of-freedom search before three-dimensional refinement. Overlap-aware residual registration (OARR) combines shared KPConv features, a folded arch-length cue, overlap-gated cross-attention, and Sinkhorn matching. Finally, dental-arch-structured hypothesis selection evaluates diverse poses on held-out reliable correspondences, while a ground-truth-free coarse-retention guard conditionally retains a geometrically reliable coarse pose. On 60 held-out jaw pairs, APCReg achieves a submillimeter mean Chamfer distance of 0.87 mm and a Hausdorff distance of 2.92 mm under this evaluation protocol, and ranks first across the six reported metrics among the evaluated open-source baselines.

</details>


### [122] [SpecF2M: A Spectral-Aware Multi-task Network Estimating Axial Length and Refractive Error from Pediatric Fundus Photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09994)
*Mengxian He,Xinyue Liu,Yunyun Sun,Wei Hao,Minqing Zhang,Lichun Wang,Shunyi Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SpecF2M，一种基于光谱感知的多任务网络，用于从儿科眼底照片中估计球镜等效屈光度（SER）和眼轴长度（AL）等近视指标。在4,359名儿童、6,966张眼底图像的数据集上，该方法在AL和SPH估计上优于CNN/ViT基线，MAE分别为0.5347 mm和0.7062 D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童近视筛查中，SER和AL的测量需要专用的生物测量设备和睫状肌麻痹验光，成本高且可及性差。眼底摄影作为更易获取的成像模态，虽然近视相关的后极部变化可见于45°眼底图像中，但这些线索具有低对比度、空间弥散和多尺度的特点，且AL、球镜（SPH）和柱镜（CYL）共享部分重叠但非完全相同的解剖关联，这给基于眼底图像的自动估计带来了挑战。

Method: SpecF2M采用光谱感知的多任务网络架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 确定性的解剖引导增强模块；(2) 混合空间-光谱骨干网络，结合MixCNN和混合光谱学习（HSL）块；(3) 用于AL、SPH和CYL分量级估计的专家路由头。

Result: 在包含4,359名符合条件儿童就诊和6,966张眼底图像的儿科队列上，SpecF2M在AL和SPH估计上优于受控的CNN/ViT基线，MAE分别为0.5347 mm和0.7062 D。分量级分析还揭示了不对称的任务耦合，即CYL与眼底衍生近视模式的相关性弱于AL/SPH。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于眼底图像的儿科近视指标估计具有可行性，支持面向筛查的应用场景。但算法在部署前仍需外部验证。

Abstract: Spherical Equivalent Refraction (SER) and Axial Length (AL) are core indicators for pediatric myopia screening, yet their measurements require dedicated biometry and cycloplegic refraction. Fundus photography offers an accessible imaging modality, as myopia-related posterior-pole changes are visible in 45$^\circ$ fundus images. However, these cues are often low-contrast, spatially diffuse, and multi-scale. Moreover, AL, Sphere (SPH), and Cylinder (CYL) share partially overlapping but non-identical anatomical correlates. We propose SpecF2M, a spectral-aware multi-task network for estimating AL and SER components from pediatric fundus photographs. SpecF2M integrates a deterministic anatomy-guided enhancement module, a hybrid spatial--spectral backbone combining MixCNN and Hybrid Spectral Learning (HSL) blocks, and an expert-routing head for component-level estimation of AL, SPH, and CYL. On a pediatric cohort of 4,359 eligible child visits and 6,966 fundus images, SpecF2M outperforms controlled CNN/ViT baselines for AL and SPH estimation, achieving MAEs of 0.5347 mm and 0.7062 D, respectively. Component-level analysis further reveals asymmetric task coupling, where CYL exhibits weaker association with fundus-derived myopic patterns than AL/SPH. These results support fundus-based, screening-oriented estimation of pediatric myopia indicators, while external validation remains necessary before deployment.

</details>


### [123] [Structural Guidance for Unified Joint Demosaicing and Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09995)
*Qixin Zheng,Ping Chen,Qiangqiang Shen,Haijin Zeng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构引导的联合去马赛克与去噪统一修复框架，通过向CFA感知的图像修复网络注入预训练结构先验知识，利用轻量级适配器弥合稀疏噪声传感器数据与自然图像预训练域之间的领域差距，在多个CFA模式与噪声水平下均优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一修复网络虽然显式建模了CFA几何结构，但主要依赖像素级监督，导致在边缘、重复纹理和摩尔纹等局部证据不可靠的区域易出现结构退化。作者归因于缺乏超越像素级重建监督的显式结构引导。

Method: 提出结构引导统一修复框架：(1) 模型接收统一五通道输入（原始马赛克、CFA掩码、噪声水平图）；(2) SwinIR修复分支在CFA条件调制下重建像素细节；(3) 并行结构推理分支从稀疏伪RGB观测中提取互补结构线索；(4) 引入轻量级可训练适配器弥合稀疏噪声传感器数据与自然图像预训练域之间的领域差距，并以残差方式融合结构和修复特征；(5) 共享解码器联合预测修复RGB图像和辅助干净马赛克，在图像域和传感器域同时提供监督。

Result: 在多种CFA模式和噪声水平上的大量实验表明，该方法相比最先进的统一方法和CFA特定方法均取得了一致的性能提升，证明适配后的结构先验能增强鲁棒的相机图像修复。

Conclusion: 结构先验知识的注入结合轻量级域适配，可有效弥补现有统一修复框架中缺乏显式结构引导的不足，提升边缘、纹理和摩尔纹等困难区域的修复质量，并具有良好的跨CFA泛化能力。

Abstract: Joint demosaicing and denoising is a fundamental step in camera image signal processing, yet remains challenging because different Bayer-like color filter arrays (CFAs) and sensor noise jointly corrupt both color sampling and image content. Existing unified restoration networks explicitly model CFA geometry but are still driven primarily by pixel-level supervision, making them prone to structural degradation around edges, repetitive textures, and moiré patterns where local evidence is unreliable. We attribute this limitation partly to the absence of explicit structural guidance beyond pixel-level reconstruction supervision. Motivated by this observation, we propose a structural-guided unified restoration framework that injects pretrained structural knowledge into CFA-aware image restoration. Our model receives a unified five-channel observation consisting of the raw mosaic, CFA masks, and a noise-level map. A SwinIR restoration branch reconstructs pixel details under CFA-conditioned modulation, while a parallel structural reasoning branch extracts complementary structural cues from a sparse pseudo-RGB observation. To bridge the substantial domain gap between sparse noisy sensor data and the natural-image pretraining domain of the structural encoder, we introduce a lightweight trainable adapter before residually fusing structural and restoration features. A shared decoder jointly predicts the restored RGB image and an auxiliary clean mosaic, providing supervision in both image and sensor domains. Extensive experiments across multiple CFA patterns and noise levels demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art unified and CFA-specific methods, indicating that adapted structural priors can enhance robust camera image restoration. The source codes and dataset are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [124] [Energy and Performance Benchmarking of Deep Learning Models for Breast Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09996)
*Samar Garrab,Ghada Achour*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文对七种深度学习模型（CNN、Transformer及混合模型）在两个医学数据集（乳腺超声和BreakHis 400X）上进行乳腺癌检测比较分析，同时评估训练和推理阶段的CO2排放。结果表明EfficientNet和ResNet性能优异但碳排放较高，DeiT-Tiny在超声数据集上取得最佳精度-能耗平衡，而在BreakHis上ViT和Swin表现最好，没有单一架构类别在两类数据集上持续占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型复杂度不断增加，其在医学图像分析中的环境足迹日益成为担忧。多数研究仅关注模型性能指标，忽视了训练和推理过程带来的碳排放等环境成本。本文旨在通过同时评估性能与环境影响，为医学应用中的模型选择提供更全面的依据。

Method: 该研究在两个医学图像数据集（Breast Ultrasound和BreakHis 400X）上对七种深度学习模型进行比较分析，架构涵盖CNN（如ResNet、EfficientNet、DenseNet121）、Transformer（如ViT、DeiT-Tiny）以及混合模型（如Swin）。除常规性能指标外，还评估了各模型在训练和推理阶段的CO2排放量。

Result: EfficientNet和ResNet在两个数据集上均稳定表现出较强的分类性能，但伴随更高的CO2排放。DeiT-Tiny等Transformer在超声数据集上表现具有竞争力，而DenseNet121准确率较低。在Breast Ultrasound数据集上，DeiT在准确率与能耗之间取得了最佳平衡；在BreakHis数据集上，ViT和Swin模型表现最优。综合来看，没有任何单一架构类别能在两个数据集上同时持续占优。

Conclusion: 研究强调在医学应用中选取模型时，应同时综合考虑性能表现、碳排放和数据集特性三个维度，而非仅仅追求准确率。由于不同架构在不同数据集上各有优劣，模型选择应结合具体应用场景进行个性化权衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have greatly improved breast cancer detection, enabling more accurate and timely diagnosis. Deep learning (DL) models show strong potential for medical image analysis; however, as their architectural complexity increases, their environmental impacts are becoming a growing concern. In this paper, we present a comparative analysis of seven DL models for breast cancer detection on two medical datasets: Breast Ultrasound and BreakHis 400X. The evaluated architectures range from Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and transformers to hybrid models. In addition to performance metrics, we assess CO2 emissions during both training and inference. Our results show that EfficientNet and ResNet consistently deliver strong performance, although with higher CO2 emissions. The selected transformers, such as DeiT-Tiny, perform competitively on both datasets, whereas DenseNet121 achieves lower accuracy. On the Breast Ultrasound Dataset, DeiT provides the most favourable balance between accuracy and energy consumption, whereas on the BreakHis dataset, the ViT and Swin models achieve the best results. Overall, our findings indicate that no single architecture category from the evaluated ones consistently dominates across the two selected datasets. Our results highlight the importance of jointly considering performance, emissions, and dataset characteristics when selecting models for medical applications.

</details>


### [125] [Robustness of transferability estimation metrics for medical imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09999)
*Niclas Claßen,Théo Sourget,Dovile Juodelyte,Rob van der Goot,Veronika Cheplygina*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究了医学影像领域中迁移学习可迁移性估计（TE）指标的稳健性问题，发现目标数据集的微小变化和评估指标的选择会显著影响TE指标的排名结果，且TE指标与参考排名之间的一致性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学影像的迁移学习中，选择预训练源模型对目标数据集性能影响巨大，但现有TE指标在不同实验设置下结果相互矛盾，且多数TE指标专为自然图像设计并优化准确性，而医学影像更关注类别不平衡下的稳健指标。

Method: 通过构建不同样本量和随机种子的微型目标数据集群体，将目标数据集作为孤立变量进行研究，并同时探究评估指标选择对参考排名的影响。

Result: 研究发现目标数据集的微小修改会改变排名；评估指标的选择会影响参考排名从而影响TE指标的评价；总体而言，TE指标与参考排名之间的一致性较低。

Conclusion: TE指标在医学影像场景中的可靠性存疑，其性能高度依赖于实验设置中的数据集细节和评估指标选择，需要更稳健的TE方法。

Abstract: In transfer learning, the choice of source model largely influences the performance on a target dataset. Still, selecting a fitting source remains a challenging task, especially in medical imaging where one has to decide between models pre-trained on off-the-shelf options, such as ImageNet, and domain specific datasets. Transferability estimation (TE) metrics address this problem by aiming to predict the best performing source model in a computationally cost effective way. However, previous work has reported conflicting TE metric performances due to differences in experimental setups. Moreover, most TE metrics are designed for and evaluated on natural images, while being optimized for accuracy, whereas in medical imaging metrics that are more robust to class imbalance are typically used. We study the impact of varying the target dataset as an isolated factor, by constructing miniature populations of different sample sizes and random seeds. In addition, we investigate the influence of the evaluation metric used to obtain the reference ranking. We find that small modifications to the target dataset change the rankings. Furthermore, we show that the choice of evaluation metric affects the reference rankings and therefore the evaluation of TE metrics. Overall, we observe a low agreement between rankings from TE metrics and reference. The code, model checkpoints and data splits used in this work are available through https://github.com/niclasclassen/robustness-of-transferability-estimation-metrics-for-medical-imaging.

</details>


### [126] [Pre- to Post-Contrast Synthesis of Breast DCE-MRI using Latent Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10000)
*Sina Amirrajab,Zohaib Salahuddin,Henry C Woodruff,Philippe Lambin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种潜空间桥匹配（LBM）框架，用于从增强前图像合成乳腺DCE-MRI的峰值增强图像，通过在配对的增强前与峰值增强VAE潜变量之间学习条件桥而非从高斯噪声生成，肿瘤条件化变体在DUKE验证集上优于预对比条件化及LDM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCE-MRI是乳腺癌成像的核心手段，但钆造影剂的使用增加了扫描负担，促使研究者探索减少或替代造影剂的方案，包括合成对比增强图像的方法。

Method: 提出潜空间桥匹配（LBM）框架：不采用传统LDM从高斯噪声出发的方式，而是在配对的增强前与峰值增强VAE潜变量之间学习条件桥。潜空间UNet从中间桥状态预测到峰值增强潜变量的剩余修正量，通过迭代细化完成生成，同时保持轨迹锚定于患者特定解剖结构。评估了两种LBM条件化变体（肿瘤条件化和预对比条件化）。

Result: 在91例DUKE验证集上，肿瘤条件化将MSE从1.023降至0.940，FRD从7.523降至4.716，肿瘤SSIM从0.355提升至0.429，且优于LDM基线。

Conclusion: 潜空间桥匹配是一种有前景的以增强前图像为锚的虚拟对比增强生成框架，但仍需进一步验证泛化能力，并消除推理时对真实肿瘤掩膜的依赖。

Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (DCE-MRI) is central to breast cancer imaging, but gadolinium administration increases scan burden and motivates contrast-reduced alternatives, including synthetic contrast generation. We propose a latent bridge matching (LBM) framework for synthesizing peak-enhanced breast DCE-MRI from pre-contrast images in the MAMA-SYNTH challenge setting. Instead of starting from Gaussian noise as in conventional latent diffusion models (LDMs), the proposed model learns a conditional bridge between paired pre-contrast and peak-enhanced VAE latents. A latent UNet predicts the remaining correction from intermediate bridge states to the peak-enhanced latent, enabling iterative refinement while keeping the trajectory anchored to patient-specific anatomy. We evaluated two LBM conditioning variants on 91 DUKE validation cases. For the tumor-conditioned variant, tumor masks were used as conditioning inputs. Tumor-conditioning improved performance compared with pre-contrast conditioning, reducing MSE from 1.023 to 0.940 and FRD from 7.523 to 4.716, while increasing tumor SSIM from 0.355 to 0.429. The tumor-conditioned LBM also outperformed the evaluated LDM baseline on this validation cohort. These results suggest that latent bridge matching is a promising pre-contrast-anchored formulation for virtual contrast enhancement, while further work is needed to validate generalization and remove dependence on ground-truth tumor masks at inference.

</details>


### [127] [Implicit representations are dead. Long live explicit primitives!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10001)
*Nil Stolt-Ansó,Maik Dannecker,Wenqi Huang,Andras Jakab,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文对高斯显式表示与隐式神经表示在医学图像重建中的表现进行了跨维度的全面评估，证明高斯表示在重建质量上相当或更优，同时显著降低优化时间和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经表示虽具备高保真度和紧凑存储，但依赖全局MLP导致计算成本高、内存需求大、优化时间长，难以适应医学影像向高分辨率、高细节发展的趋势。

Method: 首先从理论上概述显式高斯基元相比隐式神经表示所具备的数学特性优势；随后在2D显微组织学图像和3D肺部CT两个数据集上，对高斯表示与隐式方法进行全面的计算性能基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，在所有压缩因子下，高斯表示的重建指标一致地达到或超过隐式方法，同时展现出显著更低的优化时间和内存需求。

Conclusion: 显式高斯表示兼具优异的数学特性与计算效率，是医学影像领域有前景的研究方向，值得更广泛的采纳与应用。

Abstract: Continuous parameterization of medical data has emerged as a powerful paradigm for resolution-independent image representation. While Implicit Neural Representations offer high fidelity and compact storage, their reliance on global Multi-Layer Perceptrons incurs sizeable computational costs, large memory requirements, and extensive optimization times. As medical imaging trends towards ever-more detailed, high-resolution volumes, these costs impose significant bottlenecks in the applicability of implicit approaches. Recently, explicit Gaussian-based primitives have revolutionized the representation learning paradigm by trading deep network evaluations for localized, rasterization-friendly primitives. In this paper, we present a comprehensive, cross-dimensional evaluation of Gaussian representations against implicit approaches for medical imaging applications. First, we outline a theoretical overview on the mathematical properties offered by explicit primitives beyond what is capable under the implicit neural paradigm. Subsequently, we benchmark the computational performance on two demanding image datasets: 2D microscopy histology and 3D lung Computed Tomography (CT). Our experiments demonstrate that Gaussian representations consistently match or surpass reconstruction metrics compared to implicit methods across all compression factors, while displaying significantly lower optimization times, and memory requirements. Together with the compelling mathematical properties offered by explicit primitives, these findings motivate the wider adoption of Gaussian representations and position them as an attractive direction for future research in medical imaging.

</details>


### [128] [LoRCA: LoRA Cycle Adaptation for Histology to HiP-CT Translation with DINOv3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10002)
*Yang Zhou,Edoardo Occhipinti,Banboye Kidzeru Elvis,Jishizhan Chen,Stathis Megas,Joseph Brunet,Joanna Purzycka,Theresa Urban,Hector Dejea,Sarah Amalia Teichmann,Menna R Clatworthy,Paul Tafforeau,Peter D Lee,Claire L Walsh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: LoRCA是一个基于循环一致风格翻译框架，利用共享冻结DINOv3骨干和模态专属LoRA适配器，实现HiP-CT与H&E组织学图像之间的结构保持性风格翻译，无需配对训练数据，在翻译质量和结构一致性上优于CycleGAN，并为2D组织切片到3D HiP-CT容积的非线性配准提供了有前景的预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiP-CT成像技术能够对完整器官进行多分辨率三维容积成像，为组织学提供全器官背景。然而，H&E组织学与HiP-CT容积之间的非线性配准因不同色彩空间的特征表示差异而极具挑战性。现有synthesis-before-registration方法要么依赖人工解剖轮廓，要么在无语义约束下从头训练，泛化能力受限，难以适用于软组织器官和新模态。

Method: 提出LoRCA（LoRA Cycle Adaptation）框架：一个基于循环一致（cycle-consistent）的风格翻译体系，构建于共享冻结的DINOv3骨干之上，使用模态专属的LoRA适配器学习模态特异表示，通过解码器与对抗训练进行优化。冻结骨干作为结构锚定，保留预训练的语义提取能力以防止内容漂移，且无需配对训练数据。翻译质量用FID评估，结构保真度用互信息和Canny边缘保留率衡量。

Result: LoRCA在翻译质量（FID）和结构一致性（互信息、Canny边缘保留）两个维度均优于CycleGAN。此外，在手动对齐的HiP-CT与组织学测试对上，风格翻译后的图像在MatchAnything下产生更多特征对应点，表明LoRCA风格翻译有助于提升下游配准任务的可行性。

Conclusion: LoRCA证明了一种无需配对数据、基于冻结语义骨干+轻量适配器的循环一致风格翻译方法，能有效弥合HiP-CT与H&E组织学之间的模态差异，是迈向2D组织切片至3D HiP-CT容积自动配准的重要一步。

Abstract: Hierarchical Phase-Contrast Tomography (HiP-CT) is a synchrotron based X-ray imaging technique that enables non-destructive, volumetric imaging of intact organs with multi-resolutions bridging 20 $μm$/voxel for whole organs to near-cellular resolution ($\sim$0.8 $μm$/voxel) in local regions. This offers the opportunity to bring volumetric whole-organ context to histology. However, nonlinear registration between H\&E histology and HiP-CT volumes is challenging due to the differences in feature representations of different colour spaces. Synthesis-before-registration methods have shown strong results in histology-to-MRI and histology-to-CT alignment. However, existing approaches either rely on manual anatomical contours or are trained from scratch without semantic constraints, limiting their generalisability to soft tissue organs and novel modalities. We propose LoRCA (LoRA Cycle Adaptation), a cycle consistent style translation framework built on a shared frozen DINOv3 with modality-specific LoRA adapters, learning modality-specific representations that are decoded and adversarially trained. LoRCA enables structure-preserving translation without requiring paired training data. The frozen backbone is intended to be a structural anchor that prevents content drift by preserving pretrained semantic-extraction capability. We evaluate translation quality using Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and structural fidelity via mutual information and Canny edge preservation. LoRCA outperforms CycleGAN in both translation quality and structural consistency. As a preliminary indicator of downstream registration utility, we find that style-translated images yield increased feature correspondences under MatchAnything on manually aligned HiP-CT and histology test pairs, suggesting that LoRCA-style translation is a promising step towards 2D histological sections to 3D HiP-CT volumes registration.

</details>


### [129] [MD2G-Cast: Relay-Coordinated Multicast for Scalable Volumetric Streaming over MoQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10020)
*Ruonan Chai,Yisu Wang,Zili Meng,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MD2G-Cast，一个基于Media over QUIC（MoQ）的继电器协调多播框架，通过应用感知控制层实现可扩展的多用户体积流传输。该系统利用观看重叠、接收能力和带宽条件来形成可复用多播组，共享公共基础内容并有选择性地允许增强传输。作者将分组和增强准入建模为顺序控制问题，使用PPO求解，并采用教师指导训练紧凑的继电器模型。在20到100个用户规模下，MD2G-Cast将接收端P99传输间隔保持在40ms以下，同时将聚合链路负载比Clustering减少约27%，并取得最高或并列最高的系统效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积流传输（volumetric streaming）难以扩展，因为视场重叠的接收端往往被独立服务，导致共享内容被重复传输，浪费带宽和网络资源。

Method: MD2G-Cast是一个基于MoQ的继电器协调多播框架，包含应用感知控制层。核心方法包括：(1) 联合利用观看重叠、接收能力和带宽条件形成可复用多播组；(2) 共享公共Base内容并选择性准入Enhanced传输；(3) 将分组和增强准入建模为顺序控制问题；(4) 使用PPO求解，并通过教师指导训练紧凑的继电器模型用于增强准入；(5) 使用真实MoQ进程实现系统。

Result: 在20和100用户规模下，MD2G-Cast在所有七个接入配置文件上将接收端P99传输间隔保持在40ms以下，而Rolling在大多数情况下达到500ms报告上限。在所有评估用户规模下，MD2G-Cast在同类接入下取得最高或并列最高平均系统效用，在异构接入下取得最高平均效用，并在100用户时相比Clustering减少约27%的聚合链路负载。消融实验显示随机可行动作降低效用，而确定性控制与PPO相当。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，系统性能提升的核心设计贡献在于继电器协调和选择性Enhanced准入机制，而非特定的策略优化器（如PPO）。这支持了通过协调多播组和智能内容准入来替代独立传输的架构思路。

Abstract: Volumetric streaming remains difficult to scale because receivers with overlapping fields of view are often served independently, causing repeated transmission of shared content. We present MD2G-Cast, a relay-coordinated multicast framework over Media over QUIC with an application-aware control layer for scalable multi-user volumetric delivery. MD2G-Cast jointly uses viewing overlap, receiver capability, and bandwidth conditions to form reusable multicast groups, share common Base content, and selectively admit Enhanced delivery. We formulate grouping and Enhanced admission as a sequential control problem, realize it with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and train a compact relay model with teacher guidance for Enhanced admission. We implement MD2G-Cast with real MoQ processes and evaluate it with real access and 6DoF viewing traces for up to 100 users. At 20 and 100 users, MD2G-Cast keeps the receiver-side $P_{99}$ delivery interval below 40 ms across all seven access profiles, while Rolling reaches the 500 ms reporting cap in most cases. Across the evaluated user scales, MD2G-Cast achieves the highest or tied-highest mean system utility under homogeneous access and the highest mean utility under heterogeneous access, while reducing aggregate link load by about 27% relative to Clustering at 100 users. A matched relay-control ablation separates the control structure from its optimizer, showing that random feasible actions reduce utility while deterministic control remains competitive with PPO. Together, the results support relay coordination and selective Enhanced admission, rather than a particular policy optimizer, as the central design contribution.

</details>


### [130] [When Repository Labels Are Not Image-Level Truth: A Supervision Auditing Framework for Chest Radiograph AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10084)
*Yesika Alexandra Agudelo-Londoño,Jhon Wilmer Pino-Román,Brahian Carrera Rodríguez,José Miguel Castañeda-Bedoya,Juan Pablo Gómez-López,Aura C. Puche-Sarmiento,Niharika S. D'Souza,Juan Sebastian Osorio-Valencia,Jon E. Duque-Grajales,Jazmín Ximena Suárez-Revelo,Jorge Mario Vélez-Arango,Gabriel Castrillón*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Repository Supervision Auditing (RSA)框架，用于在模型开发前评估医学影像数据仓库标签与专家图像级标注的一致性。以MIMIC-CXR心脏增大为例，发现仓库标签与专家评估几乎无一致性（仅识别出1%的确诊病例），并以专家标注数据构建DenseNet121模型获得0.853的ROC-AUC，强调监督审计对可信医学AI的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共胸部X光数据仓库的标签通常从放射学报告中自动提取而非在图像上直接验证，被当作图像级金标准使用，可能导致AI模型在不可靠标注上训练、部署性能失真。缺乏系统性的监督审计方法来验证仓库标签是否真实反映影像可见内容。

Method: 提出RSA框架，包含三个步骤：(1)将仓库标签与放射科医生审查的图像级标注进行比较；(2)刻画分歧来源（区分非提及与显式否定）；(3)基于专家标注构建精选队列用于部署评估。以MIMIC-CXR数据集中心脏增大（cardiomegaly）作为案例研究。

Result: 仓库标签与专家图像级评估的Cohen's kappa接近零，仅识别出1%的专家确认病例。分歧主要源于报告中的"非提及"而非显式否定；在仓库标记为No Finding的样本中近一半实际存在专家确认的心脏增大。基于专家精选队列，DenseNet121测试ROC-AUC达0.853。

Conclusion: 仓库标签可能不可靠地代表图像级真实标注，监督审计应当成为开发可信医疗影像AI的必备步骤，而非可选项。

Abstract: Public chest X-ray repositories are widely used to train medical AI systems, yet their labels are typically extracted from radiology reports rather than verified directly on images. As a result, repository labels are often treated as image-level ground truth without validating whether they reflect what is actually visible in the radiograph. We introduce Repository Supervision Auditing (RSA), a framework that evaluates repository-derived labels against expert image-level annotations before model development. Using cardiomegaly in MIMIC-CXR as a case study, RSA compares repository labels with radiologist-reviewed image annotations, characterizes disagreement sources, and builds a curated cohort for deployment-oriented evaluation. Repository-derived cardiomegaly labels showed near-zero agreement with expert image-level assessment, identifying only 1% of expert-confirmed cases. Most discrepancies resulted from non-mention rather than explicit report negation, with expert-confirmed cardiomegaly identified in nearly half of studies assigned a repository-derived No Finding label. Using the resulting expert-curated cohort, a DenseNet121 model achieved a test ROC-AUC of 0.853. These findings show that repository labels may not reliably represent image-level truth and highlight supervision auditing as a critical step for developing trustworthy medical imaging AI.

</details>


### [131] [BreastMammo and DenseMammo: Benchmarks for Mammography Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10271)
*Hongyi Pan,Gorkem Durak,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea Mia Bejar,Mustafa Ege Seker,Nebile Alibeyoglu,Rumeysa Guclu,Rana Gunoz Comert Bozkurt,Sibel Ozkan Gurdal,Neslihan Cabioglu,Beyza Ozcinar,Ravza Yilmaz,Vahit Ozmen,Erkin Aribal,Sukru Mehmet Erturk,Yalda Zafari,Mohamed Mabrok,Kayhan Batmanghelich,Mohammad Yaqub,Ziyue Xu,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出乳腺密度分类的域泛化框架，引入两个新数据集（BreastMammo和DenseMammo），采用前景直方图匹配协议解决多中心临床数据域偏移问题。Swin Transformer骨干在内部5折交叉验证中达到98.32% AUC，外部数据集上显著优于MixStyle和DFT-based等主流域泛化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺密度分类是乳腺癌风险评估的关键环节，但AI模型因不同临床站点（不同厂商采集设备）的成像风格差异难以稳定泛化，跨域性能严重受限，亟需有效的域泛化解决方案。

Method: 1) 引入两个新数据集BreastMammo和DenseMammo用于多视角乳腺摄影研究；2) 提出基于前景直方图匹配的域泛化框架，通过在前景区域（而非全图）进行直方图匹配来消除由不同临床来源设备引入的域偏移；3) 采用5折交叉验证进行内部评估，并在TNMammo和LUMINA数据集上进行外部验证。

Result: 内部评估中Swin Transformer骨干实现98.32%的最高AUC；外部评估中该方法的域偏移降低效果一致优于MixStyle和基于离散傅里叶变换（DFT）的域泛化范式。

Conclusion: 前景直方图匹配是一种有效且轻量的域泛化策略，能够显著提升乳腺密度分类模型在多中心数据上的跨域鲁棒性，为乳腺X光影像的泛化研究提供了新思路和新数据资源。

Abstract: Breast density classification is a critical component of breast cancer risk assessment, yet AI models often struggle to generalize across clinical sites due to vendor-specific acquisition styles. In this work, we introduce two new datasets, BreastMammo and DenseMammo, to facilitate robust multi-view mammography research. We propose a domain generalization framework that utilizes a foreground-only histogram matching protocol to resolve the domain shift issue arising from disparate clinical sources. Internal evaluation using a 5-fold cross-validation protocol demonstrates the efficacy of our approach, with the Swin Transformer backbone achieving a peak AUC of 98.32% for density classification. External evaluation on the TNMammo and LUMINA datasets demonstrates that the proposed approach consistently reduces domain shift, significantly outperforming prominent domain generalization paradigms, including MixStyle and Discrete-Fourier-Transform-based frameworks.

</details>


### [132] [Retrieval-Augmented Vision Foundation Models for Robust Leukemia Cell Classification across Multiple Microscopy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10657)
*Carlos Zamora,Hiram Zuniga,Ulises Orozco-Rosas,Kenia Picos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段白血病细胞图像分类框架，利用预训练视觉基础模型在五个异构数据集上训练，通过二分类（白血病vs非白血病）和亚型分类（ALL vs AML），并在持有数据集上评估跨域泛化能力。系统比较了三种编码器在不同微调策略下的表现，并引入检索增强分类模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床场景中，白血病细胞图像分类面临采集、染色、光照和站点协议等带来的域偏移问题，单一数据集训练的模型泛化能力差。作者希望量化领域特异性预训练的贡献，并探索成本效益更高的适应策略是否可替代昂贵的领域专用预训练。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：阶段1进行二分类（白血病vs非白血病），使用122,167张单细胞图像训练；阶段2对阶段1阳性样本进行ALL/AML亚型分类，使用69,400张单细胞图像训练。五个异构数据集标签被统一协调以支持跨数据集训练，在持有数据集协议下评估域偏移泛化。基准测试三种编码器（DinoBloom、BiomedCLIP、CLIP），采用线性探测、LoRA和检索增强分类（RAC）模块（检索top-k最相似细胞图像提供细胞形态学依据）。

Result: 该框架评估了不同预训练模型在跨域场景下的分类性能，揭示了域特异性预训练的贡献程度，并验证了低成本适应策略（LoRA）和检索增强方法的可行性。持有数据集协议还能诊断分类性能是归因于数据集特定伪影还是细胞形态学特征。

Conclusion: 领域特异性预训练对跨域泛化有贡献，但成本效益高的适应策略（如LoRA和RAC）可作为昂贵领域专用预训练的有效替代方案。持有数据集协议作为诊断工具，能帮助识别分类性能是否受数据集伪影干扰，为构建鲁棒的临床白血病分类系统提供了方法学指导。

Abstract: Leukemia cell image classification is challenged by real-world domain shifts from acquisition, staining, illumination, and site protocols, causing single-dataset models to generalize poorly in real clinical scenarios. This work presents a robust framework for leukemia classification across multiple heterogeneous datasets using a two-stage pipeline with a pretrained vision foundation model. Stage 1 performs binary classification (leukemia vs. non-leukemia) and is trained using 122,167 single-cell images. Stage 2 is conditionally applied to Stage 1 positives to perform subtype classification into Acute Lymphoblastic Leukemia (ALL) and Acute Myeloid Leukemia (AML), trained using 69,400 single-cell images. Labels are harmonized across five heterogeneous datasets to enable cross-dataset training, and performance is evaluated on a held-out dataset protocol to assess domain-shift generalization. Within this pipeline, three encoders are benchmarked (DinoBloom, pretrained on single-cell images; BiomedCLIP, pretrained on biomedical data; and CLIP as a general-purpose model) under linear probing, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), and a Retrieval-Augmented Classification (RAC) module that retrieves the top-k most similar cell images to provide cytomorphological grounding. The objective is to quantify how much domain-specific pretraining contributes to performance under domain shift, and whether cost-effective adaptation and retrieval can be a viable alternative to expensive domain-specialized pretraining. The held-out protocol additionally serves as a diagnostic tool, revealing when classification performance is attributable to dataset-specific artifacts rather than to cytomorphological features.

</details>
